<script type="module">

    import { mudaLink } from '/scripts/BlocoProduto1MudaLink.js?v=20251017-1020';
        
    
   import { buildSlick2Botoes } from '/scripts/BlocoOfertaSegmentado2Botoes.js'
	$(document).ready(function() {
        mudaLink(6011)
		buildSlick2Botoes(6011)	
	
 
            //FIDELIDADE
            var segmentadoCards = $(this).find("li.items-card")
            var maxHeight = 0;		
            segmentadoCards.each(function () {
                if(maxHeight == 0){
                    if($(this).height() == 0){
                        maxHeight = 479
                    }
                }
                if ($(this).height() > maxHeight) {
                    maxHeight = $(this).height();
                }	
            }).height(maxHeight);
            
           // $("section.nodeId-6011 ul.card-segmentado-1 ul.slick-dots li:first-child button").trigger("click");
    
    
            var stay = $("html, body").scrollTop();

       
            
    })

<script type="module">

    import { mudaLink } from '/scripts/BlocoProduto1MudaLink.js?v=20251017-1020';
        
    
   import { buildSlick2Botoes } from '/scripts/BlocoOfertaSegmentado2Botoes.js'
	$(document).ready(function() {
        mudaLink(6101)
		buildSlick2Botoes(6101)	
	
 
            //FIDELIDADE
            var segmentadoCards = $(this).find("li.items-card")
            var maxHeight = 0;		
            segmentadoCards.each(function () {
                if(maxHeight == 0){
                    if($(this).height() == 0){
                        maxHeight = 479
                    }
                }
                if ($(this).height() > maxHeight) {
                    maxHeight = $(this).height();
                }	
            }).height(maxHeight);
            
           // $("section.nodeId-6101 ul.card-segmentado-1 ul.slick-dots li:first-child button").trigger("click");
    
    
            var stay = $("html, body").scrollTop();

       
            
    })
</script>

<script type="module">

    import { mudaLink } from '/scripts/BlocoProduto1MudaLink.js?v=20251017-1020';
        
    
   import { buildSlick2Botoes } from '/scripts/BlocoOfertaSegmentado2Botoes.js'
	$(document).ready(function() {
        mudaLink(6163)
		buildSlick2Botoes(6163)	
	
 
            //FIDELIDADE
            var segmentadoCards = $(this).find("li.items-card")
            var maxHeight = 0;		
            segmentadoCards.each(function () {
                if(maxHeight == 0){
                    if($(this).height() == 0){
                        maxHeight = 479
                    }
                }
                if ($(this).height() > maxHeight) {
                    maxHeight = $(this).height();
                }	
            }).height(maxHeight);
            
           // $("section.nodeId-6163 ul.card-segmentado-1 ul.slick-dots li:first-child button").trigger("click");
    
    
            var stay = $("html, body").scrollTop();

       
            
    })

	<script type="module" src="/scripts/BlocoOfertaSegmentado2Botoes.js">/**/</script>
	<script src="/scripts/disparaDataLayer.js">/**/</script>

<script type="module">

    import { mudaLink } from '/scripts/BlocoProduto1MudaLink.js?v=20251017-1020';
        
    
   import { buildSlick2Botoes } from '/scripts/BlocoOfertaSegmentado2Botoes.js'
	$(document).ready(function() {
        mudaLink(6199)
		buildSlick2Botoes(6199)	
	
 
            //FIDELIDADE
            var segmentadoCards = $(this).find("li.items-card")
            var maxHeight = 0;		
            segmentadoCards.each(function () {
                if(maxHeight == 0){
                    if($(this).height() == 0){
                        maxHeight = 479
                    }
                }
                if ($(this).height() > maxHeight) {
                    maxHeight = $(this).height();
                }	
            }).height(maxHeight);
